# Commands · G · part 3

1,416 entries, generate-migration-rails to get. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **generate-migration-rails**: 1. Confirm the desired change (add/remove tables, add/remove/change columns, indexes, foreign keys, etc.). 2. Propose or run the appropriate `bin/rails generate migration` command. - Example: `bin/rails g migration AddNameToUsers name:string` - Example: `bin/rails g migration CreateProducts name:string price:decimal` 3. When editing the generated migration: - Prefer the `change` method (avoid `up` ([naokirin/room-based-multiplayer-poc](https://github.com/naokirin/room-based-multiplayer-poc/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/generate-migration-rails.md))

- **geopolitical-report**: Analyze how a geopolitical event impacts portfolio MOIC and identify opportunities to boost absolute returns — produces a CFO-ready presentation with MOIC impact estimates and data collection recommendations ([ganoro/equiforte-demo-plugin-portfolio-monitoring](https://github.com/ganoro/equiforte-demo-plugin-portfolio-monitoring/blob/HEAD/aef49986-be7a-4e92-bac7-8056f4f68869/claude-plugin/commands/geopolitical-report.md))
- **geopos**: Returns the longitude and latitude of members from a geospatial index. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/geopos.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **georadius**: Queries a geospatial index for members within a distance from a coordinate, optionally stores the result. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/georadius.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **georadius_ro**: Returns members from a geospatial index that are within a distance from a coordinate. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/georadius_ro.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **georadiusbymember**: Queries a geospatial index for members within a distance from a member, optionally stores the result. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/georadiusbymember.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **georadiusbymember_ro**: Returns members from a geospatial index that are within a distance from a member. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/georadiusbymember_ro.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **georef.check**: Stateless, read-only. Inspects the `IfcSite` + `IfcMapConversion` + `IfcProjectedCRS` triple and reports the survey point, project north, and the four georeferencing errors that silently break federation. The "will these models actually line up in shared coordinates?" check you run before federating disciplines. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/construction/ifc-inspector/commands/georef.check.md) · ★7)
- **georref**: Trata georreferenciamento e certificacao do imovel rural — memorial, CCIR, retificacao e averbacao — com as quatro declaracoes obrigatorias sobre o prazo de 2029, que e materia SUB JUDICE e nunca se afirma como incontroversa. ([sbroggioadv/agrario-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/agrario-adv-os-marketplace/blob/HEAD/agrario-adv-os/commands/georref.md))
- **geosearch**: Queries a geospatial index for members inside an area of a box or a circle. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/geosearch.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **geosearchstore**: Queries a geospatial index for members inside an area of a box or a circle, optionally stores the result. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/geosearchstore.md) · ★86 · 2 copies)
- **geospatial-remote-sensing-ml**: Quick reference for `geospatial remote sensing ml`. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/geospatial-remote-sensing-ml.md) · ★1)
- **geospatial-visualization**: Create interactive maps and geographic visualizations with GeoViews, GeoPandas, and tile providers ([uw-ssec/savvy](https://github.com/uw-ssec/savvy/blob/HEAD/community-plugins/holoviz-visualization/commands/geospatial-visualization.md) · ★32 · 2 copies)
- **geotechnical**: &gt; **DISCLAIMER:** Review draft for professional verification only. ([upmittal/IAHE-DPR-Reviewer](https://github.com/upmittal/IAHE-DPR-Reviewer/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/geotechnical.md) · ★1)
- **gepa**: GEPA prompt-optimization trial operations — eval, history, invalidate, resume, revert, thaw. Consolidates the three prior gepa-eval/gepa-history/gepa-invalidate slash commands into one dispatcher; resume/revert/thaw were CLI-only (crew.mts) before this merge and had no slash-command wrapper. ([astragenie/dev-crew](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-crew/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa.md) · ★1)
- **gepa-eval**: Run a per-agent eval suite under a fileLockManager guard. Wraps `bun run evals` so concurrent runs for the same agent cannot corrupt the trial JSONL store. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-eval.md))
- **gepa-history**: Print recent GEPA trials for an agent (from .claude/artifacts/crew/gepa/trials/&lt;agent&gt;.jsonl) ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-history.md))
- **gepa-invalidate**: Soft-delete GEPA trials for an agent. Supports filtering by ISO datetime (--since) and tag (--tag). Writes an audit row to events.jsonl with invalidated_by_pid, invalidated_at, and reason. Invalidated trials are excluded from future recall() calls but preserved on disk (soft-delete semantics). ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-invalidate.md))
- **gepa-optimize**: Run one GEPA optimization cycle for an agent. Generates K candidate prompt variants, scores them, Pareto-ranks results, and writes an artifact JSON. When a winner is found and --artifact-only is not set, opens a promotion PR via gh CLI. Enforces daily budget cap and no-winner streak halt. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-optimize.md))
- **gepa-resume**: Clear a GEPA no-winner streak for a specific agent (re-enabling optimization after 3 consecutive no-winner cycles), OR clear the global optimize.paused flag in gepa.config.json. With an agent name: clears the streak. Without an agent name: clears the global pause. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-resume.md))
- **gepa-revert**: Revert a GEPA champion promotion. Deletes the soak pointer for the agent from soak.json and, if a promotion commit exists on main, runs `git revert &lt;commit&gt;` (never git reset, never git push --force). Reads prior_prompt_hash from gepa frontmatter to locate the commit. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-revert.md))
- **gepa-thaw**: Remove an agent from the gepa.config.json champion_frozen list, re-enabling optimization and promotion cycles for that agent. Uses atomic tmp+rename write to prevent partial updates. ([astragenie/dev-team](https://github.com/astragenie/dev-team/blob/HEAD/commands/gepa-thaw.md))
